# Card Image Prompts

本文件用于 9 国 234 名球员的 AI 球星卡图像量产。当前只生成 prompt，不生成图片。

## Template Language

| Rarity | Reference | Visual Strength | Design Language |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Legend | assets/card_templates/sample-legend-ronaldo.png | 100% luxury | black-and-gold cinematic national glory card, ornate premium border, strongest golden light, highest rarity, dramatic stadium atmosphere |
| Epic | assets/card_templates/sample-epic-bruno.png | 80% luxury | purple-gold and national-team energy card, star-player poster feeling, strong but controlled glow, premium gem-like border |
| Rare | assets/card_templates/sample-rare-diogo-costa.png | 60% luxury | blue-silver formal World Cup collectible card, clean premium border, clear player portrait, reduced light effects |
| Common | assets/card_templates/sample-common-nelson-semedo.png | 40% luxury | white-silver clean World Cup collectible card, simple but still premium, clear player portrait, minimal glow |

## Rules

- 不要求精确复刻真实肖像，只生成“类似该球员气质的足球运动员肖像”。
- 所有卡统一使用 2026 World Cup / CAN / MEX / USA 2026 赛事视觉。
- 禁止 UEFA EURO、EURO 2024、Qatar 2022、2022 World Cup。
- 禁止能力值、攻击/防守/速度属性、称号、短文案、FUT 属性面板。

## Totals

- Countries: 9
- Players: 234
- Rarity: Rare 43, Common 148, Epic 34, Legend 9
- Priority: P2 42, P3 148, P1 23, P0 21
